Here's the bug analysis and fix:\n\n### Root Cause\nThe device ID is a **random UUID stored in SharedPreferences**. When prefs get cleared (app update, clear cache, Android storage optimization), a **new UUID is generated** → Firestore still has the old one → **false \"different device\" block**.\n\n### Why logging in with different Gmail worked\nA new Gmail has **no existing Firestore binding** → auto-registers as first-time → no mismatch.\n\n### Fix Applied\n| User type | Old behavior | New behavior |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Not subscribed** (trial/expired) | ❌ Blocked on mismatch | ✅ Auto-rebinds silently |\n| **Subscribed** (paid) | ❌ Blocked (correct) | ✅ Blocked (correct — protects subscription) |\n\nNon-subscribed users have nothing to protect, so there's no reason to enforce device binding on them. The Firestore document also now stores `previousDeviceId` and `autoRebound: true` so you can track these events in your console."}
